- **data-subject-request-preparation**: Use when a person asks to access, correct, delete or move their data and a response must be prepared. ([laravel42/berry](https://github.com/laravel42/berry/tree/HEAD/server-ts/src/seed/data/berry-work-skills/data-subject-request-preparation) · ★2)
- **data-subject-request-procedure**: Use this skill when a company needs a working process for privacy requests, or just received one and has none, and asks "DSAR procedure", "how do we handle a subject access request", "someone asked us to delete their data", "right to erasure process", "data subject rights workflow", "GDPR request handling" or "we missed the one-month deadline". Designs intake from any channel, a proportionate identity check, the system search map, review and redaction, response templates per right, deadline tracking and a request log, written as a procedure the support and privacy teams can run. ([better-isms/grcskills](https://github.com/better-isms/grcskills/tree/HEAD/skills/tristan-roth/data-subject-request-procedure) · ★62)
- **data-subject-rights**: Use when receiving, assessing, or responding to a data subject rights request under Regulation (EU) 2018/1725 (EUDPR).
